# GCSS Website — Project Guide for Claude

## What this is

Full production website for **GCSS (Global Charger System Service)** — an EV charging management platform. Real users, real payments, real accounts. Not a demo. Design polish + correctness both matter.

## Stack & layout

- Frontend: `website/frontend/` — Next.js (App Router), TypeScript, static export.
- Pages under `website/frontend/src/app/[locale]/` — locale routing for EN/ZH.
- Shared styles in `website/frontend/src/app/styles/`.
- Backend: Go + SQLite in `website/backend/` with encrypted `app_secrets` store (AES-256-GCM).
- i18n: `messages/en.json` + `messages/zh.json`.

## Pages

index, product, pricing, buy, buy/review, buy/success, buy/bank-transfer, about, blog, docs, contact, forum, faq, careers, partners, b2b, login, register, forgot-password, dashboard (+ payments / server / orders), admin (+ blog / products / orders / servers / settings / bank-accounts / bank-slips), invoices.

## Deployment

Production server **47.242.75.250** (staging domain `v3.gcss.hk`; final will be `gcss.hk`).
Manual deploy (GitHub Actions secrets unset): from `website/frontend/` run
`SFTP_PASSWORD='Gcss123.' npm run deploy`.
Backend: `node deploy/deploy-backend.js` cross-compiles Go + SFTP + systemd restart.

**Current workflow (overrides prior "always deploy" rule):**
- **Do NOT deploy to the production server.** Skip `npm run deploy` and `deploy-backend.js`.
- **After every code change, push to fork:** `https://github.com/baoshan23/Design-draft` on `main`.
- **Log the change** by updating the "Last session" bullet at the bottom of this file.

## Current state (as of 2026-04-24)

- **Pricing**: 5 tiers from official PDF — SaaS Hosted ($84/yr/charger), Custom Web APP ($300 setup + $120/mo), APP Enterprise ($16,900), Web APP Platform ($21,800), APP Platform ($34,200). 5 add-ons. Dedicated plan removed.
- **Deposit**: $200 (const `DepositCents` / `DEPOSIT_CENTS`). Platform plans default to deposit-ON; balance paid via bank transfer. Charges > $1,500 are forced to bank transfer server-side.
- **Payments**: Stripe, PayPal (Orders v2), Ping++ (Alipay/WeChat, USD→CNY via `PINGXX_USD_TO_CNY_RATE` secret), and bank transfer with admin-reviewed slip upload.
- **Orders**: 5-stage steppers (order + server), printable invoices at `/invoices?n=`, admin overrides at `/admin/orders`.
- **User servers**: auto-provisioned on payment. API keys stored as sha256 + last4 only; plaintext shown once on rotation.
- **Admin secrets**: encrypted store with write-only values, masked last-four display, audit trail. Managed at `/admin/settings`.
- **Auth**: rate-limited, email-change with 6-digit code, avatar/cover uploads restricted to `/uploads/*`.
- **Visual**: golden orbs R3F canvas + frosted glass layer behind all pages; editorial typography; `--primary-text: #8C5F00` for AA contrast on light mode.
- **A11y**: skip-nav, ARIA stepper, focus-visible rings, autoComplete tokens on forms, labelled admin groups.

## Working agreements

- Focus on visual polish and completeness over new features.
- Keep EN/ZH translations in sync when editing copy.
- Prefer editing existing components/styles over creating new files.
- **Session log**: keep only the **latest** session entry below. When starting a new session, replace the previous bullet — do not append. This keeps CLAUDE.md lean.

## Last session

- 2026-06-08 — **footer reveal (all pages) — capped the yellow GCSS panel height at `520px` so the full wipe completes in ~5 wheel notches on EVERY desktop width** (user `将最后一页，用鼠标滚轮5下，即可展开全部`). **Mechanics (measured via headless-CDP, autodebug down):** the reveal scroll distance == the panel height — the dark footer's bottom edge must travel from the viewport bottom up to the pinned panel's top (= `vh − panelTop = panelH`), 1:1 with the wheel (sticky-bottom pin, no JS). So panel height directly sets the wheel-notch count. The old `height: min(38vw, 82vh)` made the panel ~547px @1440 (~5 notches, OK) but ~760px @1920 (~8 notches, too many). **Fix** (`pages.css` `.footer-reveal` ~979): added a `520px` cap → `height: min(38vw, 82vh, 520px)` (reveal ≈ 520px ≈ 5×~100px notches on any wide screen; narrower screens stay below the cap so even fewer). Also capped the wordmark `.footer-reveal-word` `font-size: 32vw` → `min(32vw, 440px)` so the glyph (line-box ≈ 0.78×font = 343px) still leaves an ~88px yellow band above+below inside the capped 520px panel (32vw alone = ~640px+ on wide screens → would clip). Verified @1920: panelH 520, wordFont 440, lineBox 343, fits, full GCSS + copyright render; reveal ~5 notches. Mobile `@media(max-width:600px)` block (`min(44vw,70vh)`) untouched — phones already < 520 + touch (no wheel). Global (shared `Footer`), so the homepage last screen matches. CSS-only. Revert: `.footer-reveal` height back to `min(38vw, 82vh)` + word font back to `32vw`.

- 2026-06-08 — **site-wide — refreshing ANY page now jumps back to the homepage first screen** (user `全站刷新的时候，自动跳回首页首屏`). Added `useRefreshToHome()` to `components/effects/GlobalEffects.tsx` (mounted site-wide in `[locale]/layout.tsx`, so it covers every route — marketing + dashboard/admin/auth). On mount it reads `performance.getEntriesByType('navigation')[0].type === 'reload'` (with the deprecated `performance.navigation.type === 1` fallback) → only a REAL document reload (F5 / browser refresh / hard-load) triggers; client-side router nav between pages is `type:'navigate'` so it's untouched, and bfcache back/forward is `type:'back_forward'` so also untouched. On a reload it derives the locale from the first path segment (`en`/`zh`, default `en`), and if not already on `/{locale}` it does `window.location.replace('/{locale}')` (replace → no extra history entry; the resulting load is `type:'navigate'` → no loop). If already on the homepage it bails and lets the existing `ScrollResetOnLoad` pin the first screen. tsc 0. CSS/TS-only, no markup or i18n change. Revert: drop the `useRefreshToHome` hook + its call from `GlobalEffects.tsx`. **Followup (user `没有改到，一刷新总会跳转到"全球支付矩阵"那里`)**: root cause of the jump was NOT the redirect — it was a focus-on-mount bug in `components/sections/home/PaymentRequestForm.tsx` (the shared home + b2c 全球支付矩阵 CTA). Its effect `useEffect(() => { if (!open) triggerRef.current?.focus() }, [open])` was meant to RETURN focus to the trigger button when the modal CLOSES, but since `open` starts `false` it ALSO fired on initial mount → `.focus()` on the inline 申请支付方式 button scrolled the browser straight to the payment-matrix section on every page load. Fix: added a `hasOpened` ref so the focus-return only fires after the modal has actually been opened (`if (open) hasOpened.current = true; else if (hasOpened.current) triggerRef.current?.focus({ preventScroll: true })`) + `preventScroll:true` as a belt-and-braces guard. Now refresh stays at the top (then `ScrollResetOnLoad`/the redirect pin the homepage first screen). tsc 0 for this file (Footer.tsx tsc errors in the tree are a concurrent session's in-progress edit, not mine). Revert: restore the effect to `if (!open) triggerRef.current?.focus()` + drop the `hasOpened` ref.

- 2026-06-05 — **ALL scroll-linked 切屏 transitions — made them 丝滑 (smooth) with the mouse wheel via per-frame lerp smoothing; killed the steppy/janky feel** (user `所有的切屏动效都配合鼠标滚轮丝滑一点，现在体验感不好，卡卡的`). Root cause: the scroll-driven effects applied their transform/width DIRECTLY from the raw scroll position on each scroll event — so every discrete mouse-wheel notch = a visible JUMP (卡卡), plus `SectionReveal` toggled `will-change` on/off every frame (compositor layer create/destroy thrash) and `CtaScrollExpand` wrote `width` per-frame while a CSS `transition` fought it (double-ease rubber-band). **Fix = decouple the visual from the discrete wheel** with a persistent rAF lerp loop in BOTH effects: instead of snapping to the target, ease the applied value toward the scroll-derived target each frame (`cur += (target - cur) * EASE`, EASE=0.16 ≈ 95% in ~300ms), so one wheel tick produces a smooth glide; the loop parks itself once settled (`moving` flag → `raf=0`) and re-wakes on scroll/resize, so it's free at rest. (1) **`SectionReveal.tsx`** (the grey-block 叠盖 cover-rise): rewrote the `update()` one-shot into a `frame()` lerp loop with a `cur[]` per-cover state array; set `will-change:transform` + `backface-visibility:hidden` ONCE per cover at setup (was toggled per frame); switched `translateY()`→`translate3d(0,y,0)` for GPU compositing; snap-the-tail (`<0.1px → =target`). (2) **`CtaScrollExpand.tsx`** (b2c CTA banner + home map width-expand): split into `targetFor()` + a `frame()` lerp loop; `cur=-1` sentinel snaps on the first frame (no load-time collapse animation) then eases. (3) **CSS**: removed the `transition: width/margin-left/padding-inline .18s` from BOTH `.cta-banner-inner` and `.map-fullbleed` (the JS lerp now owns the smoothing — keeping the CSS transition double-eased). Native `position:sticky` stacks (`.section-stack`) and the IntersectionObserver one-shot fades were already smooth — untouched. tsc 0. NOT visually verified (autodebug down). Tuning: the `EASE` const (0.16) in each component — lower = floatier, higher = snappier. Revert: restore the prior `update()` one-shot loops in both components (per-event snap + per-frame will-change toggle) + re-add the `transition: width…` to both CSS rules.

- 2026-06-05 — **footer reveal — added a scroll-linked fade-in + slide to the yellow GCSS panel (entrance only; other interactions untouched)** (`1badfbb`; user `把最后黄色区域GCSS板块，做淡入的滑动效果，不要改变其他交互`). Added a `useEffect` to `Footer.tsx` (already `'use client'`): rAF-throttled scroll handler computes a 0→1 `--gcss-fade` progress = `clamp01((wordRect.bottom − footerRect.bottom)/wordHeight)` — i.e. the uncovered fraction of the wordmark (the dark `.footer`, the panel's `previousElementSibling`, covers everything above its bottom edge; word is fully covered while `footerBottom ≥ wordBottom`, fully clear once `footerBottom ≤ wordTop`). Sets the var on the `.footer-reveal` section (via a `revealRef`). CSS (pages.css): `.footer-reveal-word{opacity:var(--gcss-fade,1); transform:translateY(calc((1 − var(--gcss-fade,1))*64px)); will-change:opacity,transform}` + `.footer-reveal-copy{opacity:var(--gcss-fade,1)}` — so as the footer scrolls up and uncovers the panel, the GCSS glyph + copyright fade in while sliding up 64→0px, in lockstep with the wheel (no jank, no scroll-jacking). **Safety**: var defaults to `1` (SSR / no-JS / `prefers-reduced-motion` → effect sets `1` and bails), and at the absolute page bottom the footer has risen so `wordBottom−footerBottom` is large → p clamps to 1, so the wordmark can NEVER get stuck hidden (worst case = current always-visible behavior). The sticky-bottom reveal mechanism, copyright placement, footer-bottom, everything else = untouched. Same rAF-scroll→CSS-var pattern as `CtaScrollExpand`/`SectionReveal`. tsc 0. NOT visually verified (autodebug down). Tuning: the `64px` slide distance, or base p on `panelHeight` instead of `wordHeight` for a slower fade. Revert: drop the `useEffect`+`revealRef`+`useRef/useEffect` import in Footer.tsx, and the `opacity`/`transform`/`will-change` lines on `.footer-reveal-word` + the `opacity` on `.footer-reveal-copy`.

Header was `className="header glass"` (`Header.tsx` 111). **`enhancements.css` IS imported** (`layout.tsx` 13 — the old CLAUDE.md note "enhancements.css is NOT imported (dead file)" is WRONG, fixed below; it cost a long investigation) and its `.glass` rule sets `box-shadow: var(--shadow-md)` (`0 4px 16px rgba(0,0,0,.08)`) + frosted `--glass-bg` + `backdrop-filter:blur(20px)`. `.glass` (spec 0,1,0) loads AFTER components.css so it BEAT `.header{box-shadow:none}` (also 0,1,0, earlier source). Homepage escaped it via `body:has(.hero-with-video) .header:not(.scrolled)` (spec 0,3,1 → `box-shadow:none`); the pricing page's `body:has(.pricing-hero)` selector is a GHOST (no `.pricing-hero` element — documented) so nothing overrode `.glass` → the shadow showed only there. **Fix = dropped `glass` from the header className** (`header glass`→`header`); the new solid-white nav design makes `glass` (frosted bg/blur/shadow) obsolete & fighting it. Now every header falls to base `.header` (opaque `#fff`, `box-shadow:none`) = matches homepage, unified. Other `.glass` users (forgot-password `.auth-form-side`, `.orbs-glass-layer`) unaffected (only the header className changed). **Verified via headless Chromium + raw-CDP `getComputedStyle`**: pre-fix header computed `box-shadow:0 4px 16px rgba(0,0,0,.08)` from `.glass`; post-fix the pricing seam is flat (white nav → 1px border → flat content, no gradient band), homepage unchanged. **Method note** (autodebug is down for this IP): `/usr/bin/chromium --headless --screenshot` (+ `--virtual-time-budget=4000` to let `pageFadeIn` finish) and a `--remote-debugging-port` + raw-websocket-CDP `Runtime.evaluate(getComputedStyle/elementsFromPoint)` probe both work here for visual/style verification — `elementsFromPoint` does NOT reveal shadow CASTERS above the point, so query the header's computed style directly. Revert: re-add ` glass` to the header className in `Header.tsx` 111.

- 2026-06-04 — **footer reveal — FIX: panel was leaking through transparent `main` sections mid-scroll** (`343efc2`; user screenshotted the yellow GCSS showing behind the homepage MobileShowcase section + `还没有下滑到最后的页脚时，不要出现这个`). Root cause: the v1 panel was `position:sticky; bottom:0; z-index:-1` with its containing block = the whole document (body), so it was pinned to the viewport bottom for the ENTIRE scroll and showed through any `main` section with a transparent bg (MobileShowcase). Fix = constrain the sticky containing block: wrapped `<footer>` + `<section.footer-reveal>` in a `<div className="footer-reveal-wrap">` (`position:relative`) in Footer.tsx. A sticky element is clamped to its parent's box, so the panel now only pins/shows once the wrap intersects the viewport (near the bottom); while scrolling through `main` the wrap is below the fold → panel off-screen, no leak. Dropped the `z-index:-1` trick → footer `z-index:2` over panel `z-index:1` (both inside the wrap's z-index:0 stacking context). Moved the rounded-corner-seam rule `main + .footer` → `main + .footer-reveal-wrap` (z-index:0 + `margin-top:clamp(-44px,-3vw,-24px)`) with `> .footer` carrying `z-index:2`/`border-top:none` and `> .footer .footer-rule{display:none}`. tsc 0; verified live :8000 renders `.footer-reveal-wrap`. Revert: see the v1 entry below (this fix supersedes it).

- 2026-06-04 — **all pages — added a go-electra-style giant "GCSS" wordmark reveal panel below the footer** (`11e8396`; user pasted the go-electra green ELECTRA reveal + `在每一页的页脚后面增加多一个页面，内容是GCSS的字母，黑色字体，背景为黄色…交互方式完全复刻这个网站的页脚处理 https://www.go-electra.com/`). `Footer.tsx` (rendered once in layout.tsx, so it's site-wide): wrapped the `<footer>` in a fragment and appended `<section className="footer-reveal" aria-hidden><span className="footer-reveal-word">GCSS</span></section>` AFTER the dark footer. CSS (`pages.css` after `.footer-rule`): `.footer-reveal{position:sticky; bottom:0; z-index:-1; height:min(72vh,560px); background:var(--primary,#FEBF1D); flex-center; overflow:hidden}` + `.footer-reveal-word{font-weight:800; font-size:38vw; color:#181818; line-height:.78}` (≤600px height `min(48vh,360px)`). **Mechanism = go-electra's sticky reveal**: the yellow panel is the LAST DOM element, `sticky bottom:0` pins it to the viewport bottom while scrolling; `z-index:-1` keeps it BEHIND the opaque dark footer (`main + .footer` is z-index:0 per sections.css 3869) and `main` (z-index:1), so it stays hidden until the footer scrolls up off it at the very bottom → clean wipe uncovering the wordmark. Works because light mode is the only active theme (`.orbs-root` is `display:none`, so nothing at z-index 0/-1 covers the reveal except the intended footer/main). The 38vw font slightly overflows so the outer letters clip at the edges (electra look; `overflow:hidden` + body `overflow-x:clip` contain it). tsc 0; verified the live :8000 dev server renders `.footer-reveal`/`.footer-reveal-word`/`GCSS`. Revert: drop the `<section.footer-reveal>` from Footer.tsx (unwrap the fragment) + delete the `.footer-reveal*` CSS block.

- 2026-06-04 — **all pages — footer seam: ROOT-CAUSED why the last 色块's rounded corners were invisible — the footer was painting OVER them (z-index tie); fixed by sending the footer behind `main`** (user `页脚上的色块背景…改成圆角` → I first wrongly rounded the dark footer → `做反了，不是页脚的色块，是页脚上面的色块` → reverted to round the section above → `看不到圆角`). **The real bug**: polish.css 1170-1177 gives BOTH `main` and `footer` `position:relative; z-index:1`. Same z-index + footer is the LATER sibling ⇒ footer paints ON TOP of `main`; pulled up `margin-top:-44px` its flat dark top edge COVERED the grey section's rounded bottom corners (the radius WAS applying — confirmed in the served CSS bundle — but was hidden under the footer). **Fix**: `main + .footer{ position:relative; z-index:0 }` (below main's 1) so the grey section paints on top and its `border-bottom-*-radius:clamp(24px,3vw,44px)` corners reveal the dark #181818 footer through the carved notches. `margin-top` pull + `border-top:none` + `.footer-rule{display:none}` kept. Last `<section>` = homepage grey `.cta-section` (page.tsx:463, `:last-of-type` confirmed) / b2b #releases / b2c #changelog / pricing #plans. CSS-only. **Verify gap**: headless-chromium single-shot is UNUSABLE here (scroll-reveal collapses below-fold sections → page renders all-white, footer never renders dark); confirmed instead via the served CSS bundle + the stacking analysis. Revert: drop the footer `z-index:0`/`position:relative` (reinstates the bug). Lesson: a "border-radius that won't show" on an element overlapped by a later positioned sibling of equal z-index is a paint-order bug, not a stale bundle — check sibling z-index before blaming cache.

- 2026-06-04 — **b2c (+ all pages) #changelog→footer seam — deepened the rounded-corner treatment; the "square corners" was a STALE Turbopack browser bundle, not a code bug** (user red-boxed the white #changelog bottom-L/R corners at the dark-footer seam + `做圆角处理`). The generic rule `.page-transition > section:last-of-type` (sections.css ~3770, added today in `619e017`) ALREADY rounds the last section's bottom corners + pulls the footer up to fill the notch — and I verified the **currently-served** dev CSS bundle (`/_next/static/chunks/src_app_0by61.r._.css`) contains the complete correct rule + #changelog IS the last `<section>` child of `.page-transition` (template.tsx wraps every route). So the user's square corners = browser cached the pre-`619e017` CSS chunk (documented recurring Turbopack issue → **hard-refresh Ctrl+Shift+R**). To make it unmistakable AND change the bundle so the stale cache can't be reused: radius `clamp(20px,3vw,36px)`→`clamp(24px,3vw,44px)` (matches the homepage #testimonials rounded-bottom) + footer pull `margin-top: clamp(-36px,-3vw,-20px)`→`clamp(-44px,-3vw,-24px)` so the pull ≥ radius (else the top of the notch shows white body and the rounding reads square). CSS-only. Headless-chromium verify was NOT usable here (the scroll-reveal animations leave below-fold sections collapsed/blank in a single-shot capture and the footer never rendered dark — min row-brightness 143). Tuning: the radius/footer-pull clamps (keep |pull| ≥ radius). Revert: radius back to `clamp(20px,3vw,36px)`, footer margin back to `clamp(-36px,-3vw,-20px)`.

- 2026-06-04 — **home #map (`GlobeVisualization`) — real-map pan/zoom FEEL (Mapbox/Google style), no real tiles** [SUPERSEDED by the MapLibre real-map entry above — GlobeVisualization is no longer rendered, but these edits remain in the unused file] (user `https://stations.go-electra.com/ 地图的交互模式，完整参考这个网站的地图来`; go-electra is UNREACHABLE via WebFetch [socket closes — documented], so via AskUserQuestion the user chose **"pan/zoom feel only"** = keep our stylized marketing world map + country markers, just make it feel like a slippy map; NOT a real Mapbox/Leaflet tile map with station data). The map already had d3.zoom: wheel-zoom-toward-cursor, drag-pan, dblclick-zoom, +/- buttons (`handleZoom` scaleBy 1.3), reset, hover tooltips. Added the one thing d3.zoom lacks vs a real map — **inertia/momentum glide**: in the `.on('zoom')` handler, when `event.sourceEvent` is a *move, track pan velocity (`velX/velY` = Δtransform.x|y / Δperformance.now(), screen px/ms); in a new `.on('end')`, if it was a pointer drag and `speed>0.08`, `svg.transition().duration(750).ease(d3.easeCubicOut).call(zoom.translateBy, velX*220/k, velY*220/k)` so the map keeps gliding and eases out (translateBy is pre-scale coords → `/k`; `translateExtent` still clamps so it can't fly into the void). Also: `scaleExtent [1,6]`→`[1,12]` (deeper zoom), `.on('start')` sets `cursor:grabbing` only on `mousedown|touchstart|pointerdown` (not wheel) → back to `grab` on end, and cleanup `svg.interrupt()` to kill any in-flight glide on unmount. tsc 0. Tuning: `glide=220` (bigger = longer slide), `750ms`/`easeCubicOut`, `speed>0.08` threshold, scaleExtent max. Revert: scaleExtent back to `[1,6]`, drop the start/end handlers + velocity vars + `svg.interrupt()`.

- 2026-06-04 — **home #payments "全球支付矩阵" — the wordmark text inside the 4 colored pills (MoMo/Toss Pay/sezzle/tabby) was NOT vertically centered → fixed with explicit per-word baselines** (user `没有对齐，重新改` → AskUserQuestion → `文字在色块里没居中`). These 4 are the only solid-colored-background logos (inline SVG pills `LOGO_NODE` in `components/sections/home/paymentIcons.tsx`; all others are transparent PNG wordmarks). Prior same-day pass had inset the rect to `x=10 y=6 w=100 h=44` (even margins in the 120×56 viewBox → 色块 centered in the square) AND set `<text>` to `dominantBaseline="central"` y=28. ROOT CAUSE of the text-not-centered: `dominant-baseline:central` centers on the FONT em-box (ascent/descent), not the optical glyph centre — `system-ui` fallback (esp. CJK metrics) drifts it, so text floated high. FIX: removed `dominantBaseline`, set EXPLICIT baseline `y` per word, each tuned in **headless Chromium** (rendered the real pill SVG at 2× + PIL bbox-vs-pill-centre): MoMo `y=36` (0.0px off), Toss Pay `y=35` (+1.5px), tabby `y=33` (−1.5px — its 'y' descender wants a higher position), sezzle `y=35` (−1.5px); all ≤1.5px from pill centre 28. METHOD NOTE (reusable, autodebug is DOWN on this IP): `chromium --headless --screenshot --force-device-scale-factor=2` + numpy bbox diff reliably verifies SVG text centering. Confirmed dev :8000 serves the new rect (`x="10" y="6"` ×4, no old `x="1"`) → user's "still off" is a stale Turbopack browser bundle → **hard-refresh (Ctrl+Shift+R)**. tsc 0. Tuning: per-word baseline `y` (±1 unit ≈ ±3px). Revert: NOT back to `dominantBaseline:central` (that's the broken state) — nudge the explicit y instead; full revert of both passes = rect `x=1 y=1 w=118 h=54` + text y 36/35/35/35 fontSizes 24/19/21/21.

On mount: `querySelectorAll('section')`, SKIP `.hero`/`.product-hero`/`.trusted-bar`/`.cta-banner` + any section containing `.b2c-feature-stack` (b2c #features has its OWN internal sticky deck — pinning it would nest-break sticky). Below-fold sections also get `section-reveal` + a shared IntersectionObserver (threshold .08, rootMargin `0 0 -6% 0`) → `is-in` then unobserve (above-fold skipped via `top < innerHeight*0.85` → no flicker). Reveal motion (inside `@media (prefers-reduced-motion:no-preference)`): non-pinned `.section-reveal:not(.--pin){opacity:0; transform:translateY(56px)→none .85s}`; pinned is opacity-only (translate would fight the sticky offset). tsc 0; all 3 SSR 200. **NOT visually verified** — tuning levers if off: cover-shadow `rgba(...,.22)`, radius clamp, pin fit-threshold `.96`, pin `top` offset (72 / +42 subnav). CSS (`sections.css` ~2796, after `.b2c-feat-shift`): `.b2c-stack-card { position:sticky; top:calc(var(--header-height,72px)+24px); background:#fff; border:1px solid var(--border-subtle); border-radius:var(--radius-xl); box-shadow:0 12px 44px rgba(0,0,0,.07); padding:36px 48px; margin-bottom:32px }` + incremental `top` per `:nth-child(2/3/4)` (+44/+64/+84px) so each prior card's top edge peeks (the deck look); `.b2c-stack-card .feature-row{padding:0}`; 4th card `margin-bottom:0`. **Why sticky works**: body uses `overflow-x:clip` (NOT hidden) + `.page-transition` wrapper is opacity-only (no transform) — both documented sticky prereqs. Removed the `<ScrollAnimation>` fade wrappers from these 4 (their `animate-on-scroll` transform would create a containing block breaking sticky). ≤900px: `position:static` (cards taller than viewport → plain stack), padding 16/20px. tsc 0; SSR `/zh/b2c` renders 4 `.b2c-stack-card`. The 6-feature lined grid above + Global Payment Matrix below kept their ScrollAnimation. **NOT visually verified** — per-card top offsets/peek may want tuning. Revert: restore the 4 `<ScrollAnimation><div style={{marginTop:80/40/40/40}}>` wrappers around each `.feature-row` + drop `.b2c-feature-stack`/`.b2c-stack-card` CSS. **Followup (user re-sent the same request → effect too subtle)**: b2c `#features.section-alt` is forced WHITE (`pages.css` 3131-3133) so white cards on white read flat. Strengthened the card edge: border `var(--border-subtle)`→`rgba(0,0,0,.09)` + switched box-shadow to a TOP-cast shadow `0 -10px 28px rgba(0,0,0,.06), 0 18px 50px rgba(0,0,0,.08)` — in a top-pinned stack each incoming card sits OVER the previous, so the separating edge is at the top; the upward shadow makes the peeking layer behind read clearly. Autodebug unavailable on this IP so still NOT visually verified.

- 2026-06-03 — **FIX: B2B/B2C sticky sub-nav broke (regression from the page-transition)** (`bfd5410`; user `把B2B,B2C页面的二级导航，滑动时，固定在顶部不动，调回出来`). The `.sub-nav`/`.product-sub-nav` is `position:fixed; top:var(--header-height)` (sections.css 4592 / pages-extracted.css 4) — was correct. But the motion-suite `template.tsx` wraps each page in `.page-transition`, whose `pageFadeIn` animation held a `transform: translateY(0)` (via `animation-fill-mode: both`). **A transform on an ancestor establishes a containing block for `position:fixed` descendants** → the in-page sub-nav anchored to `.page-transition` and scrolled away (the header is fine — it lives in layout.tsx, OUTSIDE the wrapper). Fix: made `pageFadeIn` **opacity-only** (dropped the translateY; opacity does NOT form a containing block). Page-fade kept, sub-nav fixed again. Lesson: never put `transform` on a wrapper that contains `position:fixed` UI. Revert: n/a (keep).

New `src/app/[locale]/template.tsx` (server component) wraps each route in `.page-transition`; appended an "Arcadia motion layer" to `polish.css` (after the orbs reduced-motion block, ~line 1207): (1) **page transition** `.page-transition{animation:pageFadeIn .45s ...both}` — template re-mounts on nav so it re-fires (fade+glide-up between pages); (2) **hero entrance** staggered `heroEntrance` on `.hero .hero-badge/.hero-title/.hero-desc/.hero-buttons/.hero-footer-bar/.hero-video-wrap/.hero-media-bare` (delays .05-.42s; base opacity stays 1 as no-anim fallback); (3) **scroll-reveal stagger** `.animate-on-scroll .grid > .card/.glass-card/.model-card` fade-up with nth-child delays (.05-.40s) so grids fan in card-by-card; (4) **image hover** slow `scale(1.045)` zoom-within-frame on `.feature-row:hover .feature-image-placeholder img` + `.card-with-img:hover .card-image`. ALL under `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference)` (page-transition keyframe itself is fine reduced — base.css zeroes anim duration). NOTE: someone formalized a canonical type scale in `base.css` h1-h6 (h1/h2 weight 500, h3/h4/h5 600, p 400) + the `.grid` gap 28 / `.section` 120 — intentional, kept. tsc 0; all real routes (zh/b2b/b2c/pricing/home) SSR 200 (`/zh/product` 404 is pre-existing — no such route, `product.*` is just the b2c i18n namespace); `.page-transition` renders. **NOT visually verified** (motion — user feels it live). **DEFERRED: number count-up** (the "数字" micro-interaction) — needs a small `'use client'` CountUp component per stat; offered. Fixed `899705b` (`CounterAnimation.tsx` rewrite): SSR renders the final number (sizes the span + SEO), effect resets to `0` then counts up, `threshold:0` + a 1.2s `setTimeout` fallback so it ALWAYS runs, reduced-motion shows final. Also added `.hero-stats` to the hero-entrance stagger (polish.css). SSR now shows `9,999/1,069+/100+` (was empty). Revert: restore the empty-span component from `git show 899705b^`.

CSS load order (globals.css): tokens→base→components→sections→pages→pages-extracted→**polish** (polish wins). **`enhancements.css` IS imported** (`layout.tsx` 13, loads after the globals chain) — earlier notes calling it a dead file were WRONG; its `.glass` rule applies a `var(--shadow-md)` box-shadow that leaked onto the header (see the 2026-06-04 nav-shadow entry). **Changes**: (1) **Cards** — `sections.css` ~2391 canonical surface: dropped frosted-glass (`background:var(--glass-bg)`+`backdrop-filter:blur(20px) saturate(1.2)`+white inset) → flat `background:var(--dark-card)` (#fff) + `border:1px solid var(--border-subtle)` + `box-shadow:var(--shadow-sm)`; hover gold→neutral `0 12px 28px rgba(0,0,0,.08)` + `border-color:var(--border-light)`. `polish.css` ~141 canonical hover gold→same neutral; card-icon hover `scale(1.06) rotate(-2deg)`→`scale(1.05)` (calmer, no rotate). (2) **Buttons** — `polish.css` ~50 `.btn-primary/.btn-accent` rest glow `0 6px 20px rgba(254,191,29,.25)`→`0 1px 2px rgba(0,0,0,.08)` (kept inset white sheen + the ::before shimmer sweep); hover `0 14px 32px rgba(254,191,29,.38)+ring`→`0 8px 20px rgba(0,0,0,.12)`, brightness 1.04→1.03. `components.css` ~536 `.btn-buy` (header CTA, not covered by polish) gold ring→neutral. Gold `:focus-visible` rings KEPT (a11y/brand). Root cause: anchor jump uses the global `:where([id]){scroll-margin-top:calc(72px+16px)}` (base.css 18) so `#features` top lands 88px down, THEN `.section{padding:100px 0}` (base.css 152) pushes the title another 100px → title ~mid-screen. - 2026-06-01 — **b2b `#architecture` flow diagram — REDESIGNED from L→R "flow" into a hub & spoke layout** (`b2b/page.tsx` 141-205 replaced the `.b2b-flow` JSX with `.b2b-hub`; `pages.css` 1463-1668 replaced the whole `.b2b-flow*`/`.b2b-arch*` block with `.b2b-hub*`; added `b2b.overview.coreLabel` to both `messages/{en,zh}.json`). User pasted current screenshot + a reference (center "CORE SYSTEM ADMIN / APP·CPMS·ADMIN" node + dark "自营+运营商模式" pill; top "PLATFORM SIDE" card w/ 2×2 capability grid; left "OPERATORS" + right "USERS" cards each w/ a 3-avatar row + 2×2 tag grid; subtle curved connectors) + `将这里的布局排版设计，改成参考图的这种`. **Mapping (reused ALL existing i18n)**: core eyebrow=new `coreLabel` (Core System Admin/核心系统管理), core title=`overview.center` (APP·CPMS·ADMIN), pill=`overview.model`; platform card title=`overview.platform` + tags=`platformItems` (4→2×2); operators card title=`overview.operator` + 3 avatars `{operator} A/B/N` (Building2 icon) + tags=`operatorItems` (4→2×2); users card title=`overview.user` + 3 avatars `{user} A/B/N` (User icon) + tags=`userItems` (3). **Layout**: `.b2b-hub` = 3-col grid `1fr 1.15fr 1fr` × 2 rows, `align-items:center`, max-width 1180; platform at col2/row1, operators col1/row2, core col2/row2, users col3/row2. **Colors kept BRAND GOLD** (ref was blue — user asked for the LAYOUT, not the palette): cards white + soft shadow + gold-dot title; avatars gold-tint circles `#B07A1E` icon; tags `#FBFAF7` pill + gold square dot; core white + gold border + dark `#2C2519` pill. **Connectors**: a single abs `<svg viewBox="0 0 100 60" preserveAspectRatio="none">` w/ 3 orthogonal `.b2b-hub-link` paths (vertical core→platform `M50 32 V17`, horizontals core→operators `M41 40 H26` + core→users `M59 40 H74`), `z-index:0` behind cards (cards z1, core z2) so any overshoot hides under cards; stroke `rgba(230,162,60,.4)` `vector-effect:non-scaling-stroke`. Lines are orthogonal so `preserveAspectRatio=none` distortion is nil; the y-coords are ESTIMATED (row heights), behind cards so approximate is fine. **Responsive** (≤900): collapse to 1-col `max-width:460`, stack order core→platform→operators→users via `order`, hide the SVG; (≤460) tags go 1-col. Dropped the now-unused `Smartphone`/`ServerIcon`/`ShieldCheck` imports. `tsc` 0 (dev server not up to screenshot). Pushed `75ca36f`. Revert: restore the prior `.b2b-flow` JSX + the old `.b2b-flow*` CSS block (both from `git show 75ca36f^:`), drop the `coreLabel` key + re-add the 3 lucide imports.

- 2026-06-01 — **b2b `#aftersales` "全方位支持" — rebuilt as a full-screen orbital support diagram (replaces the 4-card `grid-2`)** (`b2b/page.tsx` imports + `supportCards` array + section markup; `pages.css` appended `.b2b-support*` block at EOF). User pasted the current section screenshot + a reference design (central gold globe tile, 3 concentric rings, small icon nodes ON the rings, 4 info cards floating around) + `参考我这个设计…重新更改布局排版，内容占满一整屏展示，加一点交互`. NOTE: this is the DOM-rebuilt version of the same design I'd earlier done as a baked PNG (that PNG approach was reverted by the user). Built with real elements so i18n keys still drive the text. **Markup**: section gets class `b2b-support`; `.b2b-support-stage` (relative, max-width 1120, min-height 600) holds: `.b2b-support-rings` (4 absolutely-centred circles — ring--1/2/3 solid faint-gold + ring--dash a dashed circle that slowly rotates via `b2b-support-spin` 38s), `.b2b-support-core` (104px gold-gradient rounded tile w/ lucide `Globe`, pulsing `::before` ring), and 4 `.b2b-support-orbit` groups each = a `.b2b-support-node` (44px icon circle positioned on a ring via `--tl/tr/bl/br` top/left %) + a `.b2b-support-card` (248px white card, icon chip + h3 + p, positioned at a corner). **`supportCards`** maps the 4 i18n items to a `pos` + a lucide `Icon` (item1 视频→Play tl, item3 论坛→MessageSquare tr, item2 知识库→BookOpen bl, item4 团队→Headphones br) — rendered via `<c.Icon/>` member-expression JSX (replaced the old `aftersalesItems`/`aftersalesIcons` SVG arrays). **Interaction**: the `.b2b-support-orbit` wrapper uses inline `display:contents` (no box → its node+card still position against the stage, but `:hover` fires from either child) so `.b2b-support-orbit:hover` lights BOTH the card (lift + gold border) AND its ring node (scale 1.18 + gold fill); cards fade-up staggered (`b2b-support-in`, `--i`); rings rotate + core pulses. **Full-screen**: `.b2b-support{min-height:100vh;display:flex;flex-direction:column;justify-content:center}`. **Responsive**: ≤980 shrinks rings/cards; ≤720 hides rings+nodes, core goes inline, cards become a static 1-col stack (the `display:contents` orbit lets them flow as flex items of the stage). reduced-motion kills spin/pulse/fade. tsc 0; SSR `/zh/b2b` renders `.b2b-support-stage/core/card--tl/node--tr`. **NOT screenshotted** (autodebug needs user-enabled preview) — node/card absolute positions (the `--tl/tr/bl/br` %s) are eyeballed from the reference, so they likely need nudging once seen (cards may overlap rings/each other at some widths). Revert: restore the `grid-2` 4-card block + `aftersalesItems`/`aftersalesIcons`, drop the `.b2b-support*` CSS + the 5 new lucide imports.

- 2026-06-01 — **b2c `#gallery` phone screens — black fringe at the rounded corners → removed** (`polish.css` `.g3d-screen` ~686). User pasted the gallery screenshot with all 4 phones' screen corners (top + bottom) yellow-boxed + `手机界面圆角位置，都有黑色线，解决一下`. Same root cause as the documented feature-image fringe: `.g3d-screen` is a `border-radius:13.5%/6.2%` + `overflow:hidden` container holding a square-cornered screenshot `<img>`; its `background:#000` bled a dark fringe through the rounded-corner AA edge, and a heavy `box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px rgba(0,0,0,0.9)` drew an explicit ~black ring that read as black lines tracing the curves. **Fix**: bg `#000`→`#fff` (white can't fringe), inset ring `rgba(0,0,0,0.9)`→`rgba(0,0,0,0.05)` (hairline grey for subtle screen/bezel separation against the now-white frame, no longer "black"). Became more visible after the prior turn's white-10% frame swap. Did NOT touch the screenshots, the `.g3d-device` frame, or `.g3d-notch`. `tsc` n/a (CSS only). Revert: `.g3d-screen` bg back to `#000` + box-shadow back to `inset 0 0 0 1px rgba(0,0,0,.9)`. **Env note**: a stray `git revert a1d1cd2` was in progress this turn (not mine — likely a hook); aborted it, preserved the polish.css edit (backed up to `/tmp/polish-backup-gallery.css`).

- 2026-06-01 — **b2c `#features` "Stunning UI Design" + "Your Brand. Your Rules." feature images — black fringe at the four rounded corners → removed** (`b2c/page.tsx` 187 + 220). User (3rd msg, frustrated `没有解决到`): the two illustrations (`/images/ui-design-b2c-2.png`, `/images/OEM.png`) showed a thin **black/dark band at all four rounded corners**. My first two answers wrongly blamed baked-in crop-marks in the PNG (the gold L-brackets/crosshairs ARE in the artwork, but those sit around the inner card mockups, NOT at the container's outer corners — verified the absolute corners of both PNGs are blank light-gray). **Real root cause = CSS**: `.feature-image-placeholder` (`sections.css` 2661) has `background: linear-gradient(135deg,#1a1a2e,#0f3460)` (dark navy, a legacy loading-placeholder colour) + `overflow:hidden` + `border-radius: var(--radius-lg)` (=20px, `tokens.css` 86). The two `<Image>` ALSO carried their own inline `borderRadius: 20`. Even though child radius == parent radius (both 20px), the child image's rounded-corner **antialiased 1px edge** let the parent's dark navy bg bleed through as a dark fringe at each corner. (The 3rd feature image, smart-ops at 286-308, was already immune — a prior session overrode ITS parent bg to a light `linear-gradient(180deg,#F1F2F4,#FFFFFF)` + `isolation:isolate`; it keeps its own `borderRadius:20` and is fine.) **Fix**: removed the redundant `borderRadius: 20` from BOTH `<Image>` inline styles (now just `width:100%;height:100%;objectFit:cover`). The square image fully covers the parent including its corners; the parent's own `overflow:hidden`+`border-radius:20` clips the whole thing into clean rounded corners, and the dark navy bg is 100% hidden behind the square image → no fringe, regardless of bg colour. Did NOT touch the `.feature-image-placeholder` base rule (shared sitewide; the empty AppSlideshow placeholder at 248 still legitimately uses the dark bg as a backdrop) nor the smart-ops block. `tsc` 0. Revert: re-add `borderRadius: 20` to the two `<Image>` styles (which reinstates the bug). **Lesson**: a "black corner on a rounded image" is almost always a dark parent bg showing through a rounded child's AA edge — check the CONTAINER bg before assuming the asset is at fault; I burned 2 turns insisting it was baked into the PNG.

- 2026-06-01 — **b2b `#architecture` flow diagram — scaled up + gradient connector lines** (`pages.css` `.b2b-flow-*`). User: `内容放大一点展示，线条做成渐变效果，分别从运营商渐变到ABC,用户渐变到ABC`. (1) **Scale up**: `.b2b-flow` max-width 1080→1180, rows `240px→284px`, core min col 300→340, node cols 104→118px, rails 60→66px, gaps up. Node circle 50→60px (+ new `.b2b-flow-node-ic svg{width:24px;height:24px}` since lucide `size={20}` is overridden by CSS), node-label .82→.92rem, tier pill .88→.98rem (+pad), core padding 32/30→40/38, core-title 1.5→1.75rem, pills .9→1rem (+pad), badge .74→.82, caption .74→.82, chips .78→.86. Tablet breakpoint (821-1100) bumped to match (rows 248, ic 50px, etc). Mobile (≤820) untouched. (2) **Gradient lines**: replaced the solid `--wire` with `--wire-from rgba(214,158,0,.7)` (strong, tier/core side) + `--wire-to rgba(214,158,0,.12)` (faint, node side). The feeder is now drawn via `background-image: linear-gradient(to right, …); background-size:100% var(--wire-w); background-position:0 50%; background-repeat:no-repeat` (a 3px horizontal bar that is itself a left→right colour gradient — replaces the old vertical-gradient band trick). Direction is per-rail so it always fades TOWARD the A/B/N nodes: left rail `to right, --wire-to, --wire-from` (faint at nodes-left, strong at core-right); right rail `to right, --wire-from, --wire-to` (strong at core-left, faint at nodes-right). The branch corner boxes (::before/::after) now use `--wire-to` (faint node-end) for their borders so they blend into the faded end. `--wire-w` 2→3px, `--wire-r` 12→14px. Note: border-image can't do a gradient + keep border-radius, so the rounded branches stay a flat faint tone while the straight feeder carries the visible gradient — reads correctly as "运营商/用户 → ABC". tsc 0; SSR `/zh/b2b` renders. Not screenshotted (autodebug needs user-enabled preview). Revert: restore the solid `--wire` background band, the smaller size values, and branch borders back to `var(--wire)`. **Followup (same day)**: user screenshot red-boxed the left rail — `这里的线条被挡住了` (lines invisible/blocked). Root cause = the faint gradient end `--wire-to` was `rgba(214,158,0,0.12)`, basically transparent on the near-white section, so the branch elbows toward the A/B/N nodes (which use `--wire-to` for their border colour) vanished. NOT an actual z-index/overlap block — just too faint. Fix: `--wire-to 0.12→0.4` (still-visible light gold) and `--wire-from 0.7→0.85`; gradient range now 0.85→0.4 so the whole line stays visible while still reading as a fade toward the nodes. **Followup #2 (same day, user re-sent the EXACT same screenshot + same `这里的线条被挡住了`)**: "被挡住" literally = COVERED, not faint — re-read it correctly the 2nd time. Real root cause: the white tier pill (`.b2b-flow-tier`, `position:absolute; left:50%; translateX(-50%)`, z-index 2, opaque white bg) is WIDER than the narrow ~66px rail (EN "Operator" pill ≈ 100px), so it overflowed and sat ON TOP of the branch-merge elbows, hiding them. Asymmetry the screenshot showed (left lines gone, right lines visible) = "Operator" (long) made a wider pill than "User" (short). Fix: (a) rails widened + made FIXED `160px` (was `minmax(66px,.75fr)`) so the centred pill (~95px → spans x≈33-127px) can't reach the merge bus; (b) merge bus pulled in to the node side — branch `::before/::after` `width:18%→12%` (bus at 12%×160≈19px, well left of the pill's 33px left edge); (c) node groups anchored toward their rails (`.b2b-flow-nodes--op{align-items:flex-end}`, `--user{align-items:flex-start}`) so circles sit next to where the lines start (else the now-wider node columns left a big circle↔line gap) — mobile overrides this back to `align-items:center` for the wrapped row; (d) `--wire-to 0.4→0.5`, `--wire-from 0.85→0.9`; (e) tier pill trimmed `padding 9/17→8/15`, font `.98→.95rem`. Tablet rails also fixed (`132px`). tsc 0. STILL not screenshotted (autodebug needs the user to enable preview on their IP — could not self-verify either fix visually). **If it's STILL wrong after this**: the next suspect is the user's browser serving a stale Turbopack CSS bundle (documented: content-addressed bundles persist across dev restarts) — have them hard-refresh (Ctrl+Shift+R); if still wrong, the dev file-watcher likely died (inotify) → kill `next dev`, `rm -rf .next`, restart. Revert this followup: rails back to `minmax(66px,.75fr)`, branch width 12%→18%, drop the node `align-items` anchors.

User: `所以投影效果去掉，中间卡片增加粗边线10%的浅黄色，线条改成圆角`. (1) **Shadows removed**: `box-shadow:none` on `.b2b-flow-node-ic` (rest+hover), `.b2b-flow-tier`, `.b2b-flow-core` (rest+hover), `.b2b-flow-pill:hover`; hover feedback now = transform + border/bg only; trimmed `box-shadow` from those `transition` lists. (2) **Core border**: `.b2b-flow-core` `1px solid var(--border-subtle)` + inset ring → `4px solid rgba(254,191,29,0.1)` (literal "10% 浅黄色" = alpha .1 → soft cream frame on white; bump alpha not width if user wants it bolder). Hover → border-color `rgba(254,191,29,0.22)`. At the narrow rail width (~65-90px) the 18% box ≈ the radius so the elbow renders as a smooth near-quarter-curve (intended, reference-like). Mobile `.b2b-flow-wire{display:none}` + `.b2b-flow-rail::before` vertical connector unchanged. tsc 0; SSR `/zh/b2b` renders the span. Not screenshotted (autodebug needs user-enabled preview). - 2026-06-01 — **b2b `#architecture` "OCPP CPMS 系统平台版 – B2B" — rebuilt the tiered "shelf" diagram as a horizontal left→right FLOW diagram** (`b2b/page.tsx` 145-216 replaced the `.b2b-arch-diagram` JSX; `pages.css` 1463-1827 replaced the whole `.b2b-arch-*` block with new `.b2b-flow-*` styles). User refs: screenshot 1 red-boxed the existing 4-band vertical stack (平台方 / 运营商 / APP·CPMS·ADMIN center / 用户); screenshot 2 was a Quiety-style flow graphic (3 icon nodes on the left elbow-merge into a labeled "Collab" pill on the connecting line → a big rounded center card holding hashtag pills → "Connect" pill → branches out to 3 avatar nodes on the right). Asked `改成这种风格的层级图`. **New mapping** (Operators → Platform core → Users): left = 3 operator nodes (运营商 A/B/N, `Building2` icon); left rail = elbow SVG wire + "运营商" tier pill sitting on the line; center = platform core card (APP·CPMS·ADMIN badge row + "平台方" title + 4 `platformItems` gold pills + `model` caption); right rail = mirrored wire + "用户" tier pill; right = 3 user nodes (用户 A/B/N, `User` icon). **No content lost**: `operatorItems` (4) + `userItems` (3) render as muted secondary chips in a 2nd grid row under each side group; all three tier labels still present (运营商/平台方/用户 ); A/B/N instances kept as the side nodes. **Layout** = CSS grid, 5 cols `minmax(104px,.85fr) minmax(60px,.7fr) minmax(300px,1.9fr) minmax(60px,.7fr) minmax(104px,.85fr)`, 2 rows `240px auto`; core spans `grid-row:1/span 2` so it's the tall hero; rails + node cols live in row1, chips in row2 (`grid-column:1/3` op, `4/6` user). **Wire alignment trick**: node columns use `justify-content:space-around` inside the fixed 240px row1 track → the 3 node centers land at exactly 16.7% / 50% / 83.3%. The rail `<svg viewBox="0 0 100 100" preserveAspectRatio="none">` stretches to the same 240px (align-items:stretch fills the row track) so its path coords `M0 16.7 H14 … M14 16.7 V83.3 M14 50 H100` (left) / mirrored at x=86 (right) line up with the node centers. `vectorEffect="non-scaling-stroke"` keeps stroke width uniform despite the non-uniform x-stretch; elbow paths are H/V-only so the stretch never distorts a diagonal. Wire color `rgba(214,158,0,.42)`. **A11y/contrast**: gold pill / chip / badge text uses `var(--primary-text,#8C5F00)` (the site's AA token) NOT `--primary-dark` (#E6A817, too light on the gold-soft bg). Nodes/tier pills/core are `#fff` + gold border + soft shadow on the white section. **Responsive**: tablet (821-1100px) shrinks tracks + fonts; mobile (≤820px) flips `.b2b-flow` to `display:flex;flex-direction:column` and uses `order:1..7` to stack operators→opChips→leftRail→core→rightRail→userChips→users, hides the SVG wires (`.b2b-flow-wire{display:none}`) and draws a 2px vertical gold connector via `.b2b-flow-rail::before`, tier pills go `position:relative`; node columns become horizontal wrap rows. Dark-theme overrides flip node/tier/core bg to `--dark-card`. Reduced-motion zeroes the fade-up entrance anims (`b2b-flow-fade`, staggered by `--i`). **Verify gap**: tsc 0 + SSR fetch of `/zh/b2b` shows all `.b2b-flow-*` classes rendering — but could NOT screenshot (autodebug needs the user to enable preview on their IP); visual alignment of the wires to node centers is reasoned, not eyeballed. If the wire endpoints look off, the lever is the `240px` row1 height vs node intrinsic height (~72px×3=216) — space-around math assumes equal thirds, so keep node total < 240. Revert: restore the prior `.b2b-arch-diagram` JSX + the `.b2b-arch-*` CSS block (both from `git show HEAD:` for the two files). Did NOT touch i18n (reused all existing `b2b.overview.*` keys unchanged) or any other b2b section.

- 2026-05-30 — **b2c `#dashboard` Smart-Ops image — removed 6 decorative L-bracket / crop-mark elements baked into the PNG** (`public/images/smart-ops-dashboard-4.png`, 1332×1000 RGBA, 188 KB → 187 KB). User screenshot red-boxed FOUR corners of the dashboard preview + four corners of the `#gallery` row; asked why the rounded corners had a black border line. Investigation showed the marks were NOT a CSS rendering issue — the `feature-image-placeholder` in `b2c/page.tsx` 286-307 is a flat `borderRadius:20, overflow:hidden, boxShadow:none` div with no border, the L-marks are part of the artwork itself (white crop-mark brackets at each corner of the floating "Order Total" yellow card, plus a yellow L-frame at the dashboard's top-left, plus a white "+" cross structure flanking the "Business" button). User picked option 1 of three offered (redo PNG / CSS overlay / crop). **Method**: Python + Pillow + numpy. For each of 6 regions, built a pixel-level mask (`is_strict_white >=252` OR `is_yellow` rgb(254,191,29)±) and iteratively replaced each masked pixel with the mean of its 4-neighbour NON-masked pixels until no masked pixels remain (~50 passes for the small bbox cases). For the dash_TL yellow L bracket the arms were too long for the iterative approach to look natural (4 px × 665 px vertical line at x=198-201, y=168-833 + 4 px × 184 px horizontal line at y=190-193, x=178-361 + a square outline at the corner) — handled each arm by a direct cross-axis copy (`arr[y, 198:202] = arr[y, 188]` for the vertical arm, sampling gray page-bg 5px to the left; `arr[190:194, x] = arr[183, x]` for the horizontal arm). The bracket vertical arm also continues BELOW the Business button at x=198-201, y=920-999 — handled by the same direct-copy approach. **Critical pitfall** (cost 2 wasted passes): first iteration used `is_white(>=240)` which classified the dashboard's near-white cream panel bg (RGB 255,253,248) as bracket pixels; the iterative inpaint then sampled the adjacent yellow bracket arm and spread yellow into the cream panel, turning the area around the Business button bright yellow. Fix: tightened the strict-white classifier to `>=252` (cream still has G=253, B=248 — strict white catches only `255,255,255` true-bracket pixels). Lesson: when inpainting near a sharp color boundary, your classifier must be narrow enough to distinguish bracket-color from any near-bg color that the algorithm could mistakenly absorb. **Bbox coords**: card brackets at (342,40)-(410,90) TL, (920,40)-(990,90) TR, (340,230)-(410,310) BL, (920,230)-(995,310) BR; dash_TL square (170,165)-(215,200); business cross handled in three tight rects (60,800)-(200,840) top horizontal, (175,860)-(220,920) verticals, (60,920)-(200,960) bottom horizontal. **Verification**: rendered at displayed 666×500 size — all 6 elements gone, no visible patching seams, image reads naturally. Did NOT touch `b2c/page.tsx` markup or any CSS — the JSX `feature-image-placeholder` chrome is correct as-is. Did NOT regenerate or replace other dashboard mockup PNGs (the file path `smart-ops-dashboard-4.png` is the v4 used by the `Smart Operations Center` row only). Production file size dropped marginally (188475 → 186873 bytes) because the inpaint smoothed out high-frequency detail that compresses better. **Revert path**: `git checkout HEAD~1 -- website/frontend/public/images/smart-ops-dashboard-4.png` once committed; pre-commit backup also saved at `/tmp/_smart_ops_backup_original.png` (ephemeral, /tmp). **Followup** (not done this turn): user mentioned the `#gallery` section below the dashboard had similar corner marks in their original screenshot — those are likely also baked into a different PNG (the `Gallery3D` row uses CSS-rendered phone mockups, NOT a PNG, so the marks user saw there must be in some other artwork; if user surfaces it again, scan the gallery section for image assets and apply the same inpaint flow).

- 2026-05-30 — **b2c `#features` cell hover: dropped the `box-shadow` lift** (`sections.css` 409-414). User: `交互时，卡片无投影`. The 6 cards in "现代化 EV 充电的强大功能" had `.b2c-feature-cell:hover { transform: translateY(-4px); box-shadow: var(--shadow-md) }` — at rest no shadow (border only), on hover a soft drop shadow appeared along with the lift, matching the rest of the site's card pattern. Now the shadow is gone (`box-shadow: none`) and the `translateY(-4px)` lift is the only interaction signal. The icon-tile gold-flip (`.b2c-feature-cell:hover .feature-card-icon { background: var(--primary); border-color: transparent; box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(254,191,29,.35) }` at line ~460, set by the prior `a8ad8f6` commit) is unchanged — that small gold glow on the 52px icon tile is intentional (the user's earlier "黄色 only on hover" instruction) and is scoped to the inner icon, not the card itself. Updated the comment block above the hover rule (was `No shadow at rest (border only); shadow appears on hover, matching the other cards across the site.` — that was the old behavior and now wrong) → new comment cites the user request. Did NOT touch the rest-state rule (`background:var(--dark-card); border:1px solid var(--border-subtle); border-radius:var(--radius-lg); box-shadow:none`) or the `transition: transform .25s ease, box-shadow .25s ease` (the transition still exists so future shadow tweaks would animate — cheap to leave). Did NOT touch the universal `.card:hover, .glass-card:hover, .feature-card:hover { box-shadow: ... }` rule in `polish.css` 141-150 — that selector doesn't match `.b2c-feature-cell` (different class name), so no spillover. `tsc` 0. Revert: change `box-shadow: none` back to `box-shadow: var(--shadow-md)` and restore the original comment. Prior session's `#gallery` featured-2nd-phone-collapses-on-row-hover trick + `#features` white-tile-icon-flip (`a8ad8f6`) still in place — untouched this turn.

- 2026-05-30 — **b2c `#gallery` — featured 2nd phone collapses back to row size when ANY phone in the row is hovered** (`polish.css` 612-629). User: `这里的手机界面做交互时，原来放大的缩小成跟其他一样小，只有鼠标悬停的才会放大`. The static-state featured rule `.g3d-phone:nth-child(2) { width: 310px; transform: translateY(-20px); z-index:1; filter: drop-shadow(0 28px 36px rgba(0,0,0,.26)) }` made the 2nd phone hero-sized at rest. With the hover rule `.g3d-phone:hover, :focus-visible { transform: scale(1.35) translateY(-24px) }`, hovering on phones 1/3/4 enlarged them BUT phone 2 also stayed large from the featured rule — two phones large at once, busy. **Fix**: inserted a new block between the featured rule and the individual hover rule: `.gallery-row:hover .g3d-phone:nth-child(2):not(:hover), .gallery-row:focus-within .g3d-phone:nth-child(2):not(:focus-visible) { width:250px; transform:none; z-index:auto; filter:drop-shadow(0 18px 24px rgba(0,0,0,.18)) }`. **How the cascade resolves**: (1) at rest no phone is hovered → only `.g3d-phone:nth-child(2)` matches → 2nd phone featured at 310px; (2) hover phone 1/3/4 → `.gallery-row:hover` matches AND `.g3d-phone:nth-child(2):not(:hover)` matches (2nd phone isn't hovered) → 2nd phone shrinks to 250px/no-transform, hovered phone gets `scale(1.35)`; (3) hover phone 2 → `.gallery-row:hover` matches BUT `:not(:hover)` excludes the 2nd phone (it IS hovered) → row-reset rule doesn't apply → `.g3d-phone:hover` rule applies on the 2nd phone, giving it `scale(1.35) translateY(-24px)` instead of the static featured look. **Specificity math** (for reader sanity): featured rule (0,1,1) < row-reset (0,4,0) < individual `:hover` (0,2,0). Wait — the row-reset has higher specificity than individual hover, but they never compete because of the `:not(:hover)` filter — when individual hover wins, the row-reset selector simply doesn't match. Without `:not(:hover)`, the higher-specificity row-reset would override the individual hover and the hovered 2nd phone would stay small (bug). The `:not()` is load-bearing. Also added `:focus-within`/`:focus-visible` variant for keyboard nav. Source-order remains: featured-2nd → row-reset → individual-hover so the css reads top-to-bottom in the order it fires. Mobile breakpoint (`max-width:760px`) was already a flat grid reset (`.g3d-phone:nth-child(2){width:auto;transform:none}`) — touch devices don't really "hover" so this turn's rule is dormant there. `prefers-reduced-motion` already zeros the transition; the static→hover swap still happens but instantly. `tsc` 0. Revert: delete the new `.gallery-row:hover .g3d-phone:nth-child(2):not(:hover), .gallery-row:focus-within .g3d-phone:nth-child(2):not(:focus-visible)` block entirely (the featured rule + individual-hover rule sit on either side, untouched). Prior session's `#features` white-icon-tile + hover-flip-to-gold (`a8ad8f6`), b2b `#demo` equal heights, and the `#support` bento-grid (`3159866`) all still in place — untouched this turn.

Revert: restore the prior single-state block (`border: 0; background: var(--primary,#febf1d); box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(254,191,29,.35)`) and delete the `:hover, :focus-within` rule and the `transition` line.

- 2026-05-30 — **b2c `#gallery` "产品展示" — phones enlarged + 2nd phone featured by default (no subtitle overlap)** (`polish.css` 574-620 + 690-707). Two-step this turn driven by paired user msgs: (1) `手机界面放大一点点，四个的间距可以小一点` → bumped `.g3d-phone { width: 220px → 250px }` and tightened `.gallery-row { max-width: 1280px → 1180px, gap: 32px → 24px }`. With `justify-content: space-between` the actual gap is computed by container, not by `gap`; the new max-width brings the inter-phone gap from ~123px (old: `1280-32-880`)/3 = ~123) down to ~29px (`1148-1060`)/3 after the featured 2nd kicks in). (2) `静态展示时，默认第二个手机界面放大，当不要挡住副标题的文字内容` → added a new static rule `.g3d-phone:nth-child(2) { width: 310px; transform: translateY(-20px); z-index: 1; filter: drop-shadow(0 28px 36px rgba(0,0,0,.26)) }` placed BETWEEN the base `.g3d-phone` rule and the `:hover, :focus-visible` rule so hover (same specificity 0,2,0) wins on any phone via source-order. The -20px lift stays well within the gallery row's `padding-top: 80px` buffer — even on a 310px wide phone (`height = 310 × 844/390 = 671px`), the top edge lands at 60px from section top, leaving plenty of clearance above for the subtitle "查看 GCSS 在移动端、网页端和管理端的实际运行效果。". Hover state (`scale(1.35) translateY(-24px)`) untouched — when user actively hovers, the 1.35x explosion still happens (transient, user-initiated; user's constraint was about STATIC overlap). Mobile breakpoint (`@media (max-width:760px)`): added `.g3d-phone:nth-child(2) { width:auto; transform:none; z-index:auto; filter:drop-shadow(0 18px 24px rgba(0,0,0,.18)) }` to reset the featured rule inside the 2-col grid (same selector specificity, later source order → wins inside the @media). `Gallery3D.tsx` markup untouched — still 4 plain `.g3d-phone tabIndex=0 role="img"` divs in source order, no `is-active` class anymore (the `3fe4b93` click-to-enlarge state was already reverted to hover/focus before this turn). `tsc` 0. Revert: change `.g3d-phone { width: 250px → 220px }`, `.gallery-row { max-width: 1180px → 1280px, gap: 24px → 32px }`, delete the new `.g3d-phone:nth-child(2)` block AND the mobile reset block.

- 2026-05-30 — **Nav bar drop-shadow removed sitewide — `box-shadow: none` on every `.header.scrolled` variant** (`components.css` 106-117 + 135-141). User retried `没有改到，重新改` after two prior attempts targeting only `body:has(.pricing-hero)` did nothing on the actual pricing page. **Root cause**: `.pricing-hero` is not a class that exists anywhere in the codebase (`grep` confirmed only the two CSS selectors reference it — the pricing-page markup uses `section#plans.section.pricing-section.pricing-section--tabs` instead). So `body:has(.pricing-hero) .header` never matched and my "fix" applied to nothing on the page the user was looking at. The shadow the user saw came from the base rule `.header.scrolled { box-shadow: 0 4px 14px rgba(0,0,0,0.05) }` which fires on every page once `scrollY > 20` (logic in `Header.tsx` 58). **Fix**: replaced `box-shadow: 0 4px 14px rgba(0,0,0,0.05)` with `box-shadow: none` in BOTH places where it lived — the base `.header.scrolled` (line ~110) AND the homepage override `body:has(.hero-with-video) .header.scrolled` (line ~139). Used `replace_all:true` on the 4-line block since both occurrences were byte-identical. The dead `body:has(.pricing-hero)` block stays in place but is harmless (no markup matches it; pruning could nick the demo page which DOES have `.demo-hero` — left intact). Border line `rgba(0,0,0,0.06)` stays everywhere. Backdrop blur stays everywhere. Transition `all 0.4s cubic-bezier(0.16,1,0.3,1)` on `.header` (line 104) still animates the colour/border state-changes, just nothing shadow-shaped to animate now. **Lesson for next time**: when a CSS selector "fix" doesn't take effect, FIRST verify the selector actually matches the page (search the JSX for the class name). Don't trust the CSS file's history of references — `.pricing-hero` was a ghost selector that had been added prophylactically. `tsc` 0. Revert: change both `box-shadow: none` back to `box-shadow: 0 4px 14px rgba(0,0,0,0.05)` and drop the new comment block. - 2026-05-30 — **Pricing / demo nav: drop box-shadow in BOTH static AND scrolled states — only border line remains** (`components.css` 143-156). User repeated `顶部导航栏静态展示时，无投影，只有边线` with the same scrolled-state pricing-page screenshot. **First attempt this turn** (commit `d26d796`, since superseded): split the 4-selector group into static (no shadow) + `.scrolled` (shadow) — reasoning: "静态展示时" = static state, so leave the scrolled shadow alone. **User repeated the request with the SAME screenshot showing the scrolled state still has the shadow** — interpretation #1 was wrong. Correct reading: "静态展示" means "show the nav statically/without effects" (interpretation #2), i.e. no dynamic drop-shadow appearing at all. **Final fix**: re-merged the 4-selector group `body:has(.demo-hero) .header, body:has(.demo-hero) .header.scrolled, body:has(.pricing-hero) .header, body:has(.pricing-hero) .header.scrolled` into one block with `box-shadow: none` everywhere. No drop-shadow in EITHER state — only the 1px border line separates the nav from the content (user request: "只有边线").` **Lesson** (for future me): when the user repeats the same request with the same screenshot after a "fix", do not assume the prior interpretation was right — re-read the request literally. "只有边线" (only the border line) was the load-bearing phrase — if shadow remains in any state, the user will repeat. Other pages still get the scroll-shadow normally: base `.header.scrolled` (line 105) keeps `box-shadow: 0 4px 14px rgba(0,0,0,0.05)`, and the homepage `body:has(.hero-with-video) .header.scrolled` (line 134) keeps it too — only pricing+demo override is shadow-free. Dropped `'use client'`/`useState`/`onClick`/`<button>` and the `is-active` class machinery; `Gallery3D.tsx` back to a pure SSR `<div tabIndex={0} role="img">` component. Earlier troubleshooting note: when an edit doesn't visibly take effect on :8000 dev, the running `next dev`'s file-watcher likely died (container inotify limit); kill the process, `rm -rf .next`, restart `npm run dev` — and the user must hard-refresh (Ctrl+Shift+R) to bust the browser's cached CSS bundle (Turbopack uses content-addressed bundle URLs, so cached bundles persist across server restarts when content is identical). Revert: swap `:hover,:focus-visible` back to `.is-active` and restore the `useState`/`onClick` flow from commit `3fe4b93`. User: `鼠标做点击交互时，手机界面才放大，静态展示时，四个一样大`. Replaces the prior "2nd phone featured + hover lift" state (commit `4e27f53`). **Component**: re-added `'use client'`, `useState<number | null>(null)` tracks the active index, root tag changed from `<div>` to `<button type="button">` per phone (semantic for click-to-toggle + native focus/keyboard handling), `onClick` toggles `(curr === i ? null : i)` so clicking the same phone again resets, `aria-pressed={active === i}`, `aria-label={p.label}`. Default state: no phone is enlarged. **CSS**: dropped the `.g3d-phone:nth-child(2){width:300px;translateY(-32px)}` featured rule AND all the `:hover/:focus-visible{translateY(-24px)}` lift rules. All 4 phones now share `width:220px;flex:0 0 auto;transform-origin:50% 100%` (origin bottom-center so scale-up grows upward, keeping the baseline). Added `<button>` resets: `padding:0;background:transparent;border:none;font:inherit;color:inherit;cursor:pointer` so the `<button>` looks identical to the prior `<div>`. **Active state**: `.g3d-phone.is-active{transform:scale(1.35) translateY(-24px);z-index:2;filter:drop-shadow(0 36px 44px rgba(0,0,0,.32))}` — scale + small lift, on top, shadow deepened. Transition same `transform .45s cubic-bezier(.22,1,.36,1),filter .45s`. Mobile (`max-width:760px`): grid stays, `.g3d-phone.is-active` softer (`scale(1.08) translateY(-6px)`). `prefers-reduced-motion` zeros transition duration. `tsc` 0. Revert: re-apply `:nth-child(2)` + `:hover` blocks, remove `is-active` rule, change `<button>` back to `<div tabIndex={0}>`, drop `useState`/onClick.

Prior session's `Gallery3D` work (b2c `#gallery` row, final commit `4e27f53`) still in place — untouched this turn. Started this session as the original `Gallery3D` 3D cluster (4 absolute-positioned slots in back/front pairs, scene-wide parallax tilt, single-phone hover straightens to front). Walked through 4 layouts via user feedback: (a) `9506399` hover-to-feature row (`useState`+`is-active` class, default index 1 scaled+tilted, hover hands spotlight); (b) `429c11c` fully static evenly-distributed row after `静态展示时，对齐分布，无交互` (dropped `useState`/hover entirely, `flex:1 1 0;max-width:220px`); (c) `4bbf065` enlarged + hover lift after `展示界面放大一点，鼠标光标交互时，手机界面向上展示` (re-added `tabIndex`, `max-width:280px`, hover `translateY(-24px)`); (d) `4e27f53` current state mirroring user's 4-phone reference image (`参考这个布局`): bottoms aligned at baseline, 2nd phone enlarged and raised. **Current CSS**: `.gallery-row{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:flex-end;gap:32px;max-width:1280px;padding:80px 16px 40px}` (top padding leaves room for the raised 2nd phone). `.g3d-phone{flex:0 0 auto;width:220px;cursor:pointer;filter:drop-shadow(0 18px 24px rgba(0,0,0,.18));transition:transform .45s cubic-bezier(.22,1,.36,1),filter .45s}`. **Featured rule** `.g3d-phone:nth-child(2){width:300px;transform:translateY(-32px);filter:drop-shadow(0 30px 38px rgba(0,0,0,.28));z-index:2}` — fixed-width approach (NOT scale transform) so the bezel/screen scale together correctly. Hover: `.g3d-phone:hover/:focus-visible{transform:translateY(-24px);filter:drop-shadow(0 36px 44px rgba(0,0,0,.32))}` and a stacked override `.g3d-phone:nth-child(2):hover{transform:translateY(-56px)}` so the already-raised featured phone lifts further (-32 base + -24 hover delta = -56). `:focus-visible` adds 2px gold outline at offset 6 with 24px radius. **Mobile (`max-width:760px`)**: collapses to 2-col grid, both base + `:nth-child(2)` rules reset (`width:auto;transform:none`), hover lift drops to `-8px` (also applied to nth-child(2)). `prefers-reduced-motion` zeros transition duration only (transform still applies — accessible default for "phone moves on hover"). `Gallery3D.tsx` is a pure SSR component (no `'use client'`, no state) — interaction is 100% CSS. Kept the graphite `.g3d-device` + metallic `::before` rim + `.g3d-screen` + `.g3d-notch` mockup unchanged throughout. `tsc` 0. Revert path: restore the original `Gallery3D` (absolute slots + scene-tilt useState) + the old `.gallery-3d`/`.gallery-3d-stage`/`.g3d-slot-*` CSS block.

- 2026-05-30 — **Pricing `#addons` "可选扩展" cards restyle: editorial-stat layout, yellow icon tile + black line icon, vertical dividers between columns** (`sections.css` 4283-4360, commits `3e23bcb` + `619440d` + `2dd3e6f` pushed fork `design-draft-local`). Three-step this turn: (1) `3e23bcb` shipped the editorial-stat layout (centered, icon on top, big bold price under it, small grey label below) AND flipped the icon to black-bg + yellow-icon with no shadow, mirroring the "12-24 hours / 2 seconds / 40% faster" reference. (2) `619440d` flipped the icon palette back: user wanted yellow background + black icon (the original brand pairing) while keeping the no-shadow flat treatment and the new centered editorial layout. (3) `2dd3e6f` added thin vertical divider lines between cards (user reference image had red guides drawn between the 3 columns) — `.addon-card{position:relative}` + scoped pseudo-element `@media(min-width:901px){.addons-grid .addon-card:not(:nth-child(3n+1))::before{content:'';position:absolute;left:-7px;top:12%;bottom:12%;width:1px;background:rgba(0,0,0,.1);pointer-events:none}}`. `left:-7px` centers the 1px line in the 14px grid gap. Companion `@media(max-width:900px) and (min-width:561px){.addons-grid .addon-card:nth-child(2n)::before{...}}` handles the 2-column tablet breakpoint (every even card gets a left divider). At the 1-col mobile breakpoint (`max-width:560px`) no rule fires, so no dividers stack vertically. Old cards rendered as left-aligned chips with `.addon-card{background:var(--dark-card);border:1px solid var(--border-light);border-radius:var(--radius-md);padding:28px 20px;flex-direction:column;align-items:flex-start;gap:14px;...:hover{translateY(-4px);box-shadow:var(--shadow-md)}}` (from the original block 3818-3858) plus the override at 4283 which only set `flex-direction:column;align-items:flex-start;gap:14px`, and `.addon-card-icon` was a 52px solid-gold tile with `background:var(--primary)`, `color:#181818`, `box-shadow:0 0 20px rgba(254,191,29,.35)` (the gold glow). **Now** (overrides at 4283-4329, originals at 3818-3858 left intact — overrides win by cascade): `.addon-card{background:transparent;border:none;border-radius:0;padding:24px 16px;align-items:center;text-align:center;gap:18px;box-shadow:none;transition:none}` + a fresh `.addon-card:hover{transform:none;box-shadow:none}` that kills the lift. `.addon-card-icon` → **56px, `background:var(--primary,#febf1d)`, `color:#181818`, `box-shadow:none`** (yellow bg, black icon, no glow — the 619440d state). `.addon-card-body{flex-direction:column-reverse;align-items:center;text-align:center;gap:8px}` — DOM order stays label→price (a11y-friendly: what-it-is read before how-much), `column-reverse` flips visual order so price renders directly under the icon as the big stat and label sits below as the description. New scoped overrides `.addon-card .addon-label{font-size:.92rem;font-weight:500;color:var(--text-secondary,#555);line-height:1.45}` and `.addon-card .addon-price{font-size:1.6rem;font-weight:800;color:var(--primary);line-height:1.1;letter-spacing:-.01em}`. Markup unchanged (`pricing/page.tsx` 271-281 still renders `.addons-grid > .addon-card > .addon-card-icon + .addon-card-body > .addon-label + .addon-price`). Revert: restore the original 4283-4329 block from before `3e23bcb`.
